00029: def B(a,n,s,p,c):
00030:     return a.include(n,box(size=s,center=p,color=c))
00031: 
00032: def R(a,n,r,p,q,c):
00033:     if sum((p[k]-q[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-10:
00034:         return a.include(n,rod(radius=r,start=p,end=q,color=c))
00035: 
00036: def cable(a,n,pts,r,c):
00037:     clean=[]
00038:     for p in pts:
00039:         if not clean or sum((p[k]-clean[-1][k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-10:
00040:             clean.append(p)
00041:     for j in range(len(clean)-1):
00042:         R(a,n+'_'+str(j),r,clean[j],clean[j+1],c)
00043: 
00044: def finish(n,a):
00045:     return scene.include(n,a)

00218: def texture_group(name, style):
00219:     group = bpy.data.objects.get(name)
00220:     if group is None:
00221:         return
00222:     for obj in descendants(group):
00223:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00224:             if slot.material is None:
00225:                 continue
00226:             mat = slot.material.copy()
00227:             slot.material = mat
00228:             mat.use_nodes = True
00229:             nodes, links = mat.node_tree.nodes, mat.node_tree.links
00230:             bs = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00231:             bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .60 if name == "floorboards" else .76
00232:             base = tuple(bs.inputs["Base Color"].default_value)
00233:             coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00234:             mapping = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00235:             mapping.operation = "MULTIPLY"
00236:             mapping.inputs[1].default_value = (26, 1.4, 5) if style == "wood" else (1, 1, 1)
00237:             noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00238:             noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 5 if style == "wood" else 13
00239:             noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 3
00240:             links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"], mapping.inputs[0])
00241:             links.new(mapping.outputs[0], noise.inputs["Vector"])

00207: def descendants(obj):
00208:     if obj.type == 'MESH':
00209:         yield obj
00210:     for child in obj.children:
00211:         yield from descendants(child)

00213: def texture_group(group_name, style):
00214:     group = bpy.data.objects.get(group_name)
00215:     if group is None:
00216:         return
00217:     for obj in descendants(group):
00218:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00219:             if not slot.material:
00220:                 continue
00221:             mat = slot.material.copy()
00222:             slot.material = mat
00223:             mat.use_nodes = True
00224:             nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00225:             links = mat.node_tree.links
00226:             bsdf = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00227:             bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .78
00228:             base = tuple(bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value)
00229:             tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00230:             tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 5 if style == "wood" else 15
00231:             tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 3
00232:             coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00233:             mapping = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00234:             mapping.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00235:             mapping.inputs[1].default_value = (26,1.4,5) if style == "wood" else (1,1,1)
00236:             links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"], mapping.inputs[0])
00237:             links.new(mapping.outputs[0], tex.inputs["Vector"])
00238:             ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00239:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .17
00240:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = tuple(v*.55 for v in base[:3])+(1,)
00241:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .83
00242:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = tuple(min(v*1.3,1) for v in base[:3])+(1,)
00243:             links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00244:             links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bsdf.inputs["Base Color"])
00245:             bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00246:             bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .24
00247:             bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .018 if style == "wood" else .035
00248:             links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00249:             links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bsdf.inputs["Normal"])

00194: def descendants(obj):
00195:     if obj.type == "MESH":
00196:         yield obj
00197:     for child in obj.children:
00198:         yield from descendants(child)

00200: def texture_group(name,style):
00201:     group = bpy.data.objects.get(name)
00202:     if group is None:
00203:         return
00204:     for obj in descendants(group):
00205:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00206:             if slot.material is None:
00207:                 continue
00208:             mat = slot.material.copy()
00209:             slot.material = mat
00210:             mat.use_nodes = True
00211:             nodes,links = mat.node_tree.nodes,mat.node_tree.links
00212:             bs = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00213:             bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .76
00214:             base = tuple(bs.inputs["Base Color"].default_value)
00215:             coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00216:             mapping = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00217:             mapping.operation = "MULTIPLY"
00218:             mapping.inputs[1].default_value = (26,1.4,5) if style=="wood" else (1,1,1)
00219:             noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00220:             noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 5 if style=="wood" else 13
00221:             noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 3
00222:             links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"],mapping.inputs[0])
00223:             links.new(mapping.outputs[0],noise.inputs["Vector"])
00224:             ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00225:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .17
00226:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = tuple(v*.62 for v in base[:3])+(1,)
00227:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .83
